# FLOW AGENT: generate all 290 images for Effortless Exercise (Days 4–30)

## Instructions for the agent (read once, then work without stopping)

- The character reference **H** is already attached. Use it for EVERY image so H looks exactly the same each time (face, body, outfit).
- **Every image must be a UNIQUE, dynamic shot** (action moment, different camera angle, body in motion). Never a plain straight standing pose with only the hands or face changed.
- Generate the images in **sets of 24**, in the order below (Set 1, Set 2, …). Start Set 1 now.
- **Do NOT stop to ask me to continue** after a set. All approvals are already given for everything in this file. Go straight to the next set until all sets are done.
- Quality: **HD / highest quality**, sharp and detailed. Vertical **9:16**.
- Generate **one image per prompt**. Save each image with the **exact file name** shown above its prompt (e.g. `d04_s01.png`).
- If an image comes out wrong (H's face, body or outfit changed; extra fingers or limbs; any text appears; wrong exercise form; a stiff standing pose), regenerate that one image before moving on. Do not ask me.
- Images marked **WHITE** must have a pure white background. Images marked **SCENE** keep their full background.
- Two files ending in **a** and **b** are two frames of one exercise loop: same camera angle, same size, same position.
- When ALL sets are finished, send me **one message** with: total images made, any prompt that failed after retries (file name + reason), and the download link or zip of all files.

**Total: 290 images in 13 sets of 24.**
